    41  func notAuthorized(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) {
    42          if c.IsApiRequest() {
    43                  c.WriteErrOrFallback(http.StatusUnauthorized, http.StatusText(http.StatusUnauthorized), c.LookupTokenErr)
    44                  return
    45          }
    46
    47          if !c.UseSessionStorageRedirect {
    48                  writeRedirectCookie(c)
    49          }
    50
    51          if errors.Is(c.LookupTokenErr, authn.ErrTokenNeedsRotation) {
    52                  if !c.UseSessionStorageRedirect {
    53                          c.Redirect(setting.AppSubUrl + "/user/auth-tokens/rotate")
    54                          return
    55                  }
    56
    57                  c.Redirect(setting.AppSubUrl + "/user/auth-tokens/rotate" + getRedirectToQueryParam(c))
    58                  return
    59          }
    60
    61          if !c.UseSessionStorageRedirect {
    62                  c.Redirect(setting.AppSubUrl + "/login")
    63                  return
    64          }
    65
    66          c.Redirect(setting.AppSubUrl + "/login" + getRedirectToQueryParam(c))

    82                  writeRedirectCookie(c)
    83                  c.Redirect(setting.AppSubUrl + "/login")
    84                  return
    85          }

    87          c.Redirect(setting.AppSubUrl + "/login" + getRedirectToQueryParam(c))

    25  func HandleNoCacheHeaders(ctx *contextmodel.ReqContext) {

    27          ctx.SkipDSCache = ctx.Req.Header.Get("X-Grafana-NoCache") == "true"

    32  func AddDefaultResponseHeaders(cfg *setting.Cfg) web.Handler {
    33          t := web.NewTree()
    34          t.Add("/api/datasources/uid/:uid/resources/*", nil)
    35          t.Add("/api/datasources/:id/resources/*", nil)
    36          t.Add("/api/plugins/:id/resources/*", nil)
    37
    38          return func(c *web.Context) {
    39                  c.Resp.Before(func(w web.ResponseWriter) { // if response has already been written, skip.
    40                          if w.Written() {
    41                                  return
    42                          }
    43
    44                          traceId := tracing.TraceIDFromContext(c.Req.Context(), false)
    45                          if traceId != "" {
    46                                  w.Header().Set("grafana-trace-id", traceId)
    47                          }
    48
    49                          _, _, resourceURLMatch := t.Match(c.Req.URL.Path)
    50                          resourceCachable := resourceURLMatch && allowCacheControl(c.Resp)
    51                          if !strings.HasPrefix(c.Req.URL.Path, "/public/plugins/") &&
    52                                  !strings.HasPrefix(c.Req.URL.Path, "/avatar/") &&
    53                                  !strings.HasPrefix(c.Req.URL.Path, "/api/datasources/proxy/") &&
    54                                  !strings.HasPrefix(c.Req.URL.Path, "/api/reports/render/") &&
    55                                  !strings.HasPrefix(c.Req.URL.Path, "/render/d-solo/") &&
    56                                  (!strings.HasPrefix(c.Req.URL.Path, "/api/gnet/plugins") || !strings.Contains(c.Req.URL.Path, "/logos/")) && !resourceCachable {
    57                                  addNoCacheHeaders(c.Resp)
    58                          }
    59
    60                          // X-Allow-Embedding header is set for specific URLs that need to be embedded in an iframe regardless
    61                          // of the configured allow_embedding setting.
    62                          embeddingHeader := w.Header().Get("X-Allow-Embedding")
    63                          if !cfg.AllowEmbedding && embeddingHeader != "allow" {
    64                                  addXFrameOptionsDenyHeader(w)
    65                          }
    66                          addSecurityHeaders(w, cfg)
    67                  })
    68          }
    69  }

    31  func (hs *HTTPServer) registerShortURLAPI(apiRoute routing.RouteRegister) {
    32          reqSignedIn := middleware.ReqSignedIn

    34                  handler := newShortURLK8sHandler(hs)
    35                  apiRoute.Post("/api/short-urls", reqSignedIn, handler.createKubernetesShortURLsHandler)
    36                  apiRoute.Get("/api/short-urls/:uid", reqSignedIn, handler.getKubernetesShortURLsHandler)

    40                  apiRoute.Get("/api/short-urls/:uid", reqSignedIn, hs.getShortURL)
    41                  apiRoute.Get("/goto/:uid", reqSignedIn, hs.redirectFromShortURL, hs.Index)
    42          }
    43  }
    44
    45  // createShortURL handles requests to create short URLs.
    46  func (hs *HTTPServer) createShortURL(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) response.Response {
    47          cmd := &dtos.CreateShortURLCmd{}
    48          if err := web.Bind(c.Req, &cmd); err != nil {
    49                  return response.Err(shorturls.ErrShortURLBadRequest.Errorf("bad request data: %w", err))
    50          }
    51          hs.log.Debug("Received request to create short URL", "path", cmd.Path)
    52          shortURL, err := hs.ShortURLService.CreateShortURL(c.Req.Context(), c.SignedInUser, cmd)
    53          if err != nil {
    54                  return response.Err(err)
    55          }
    56
    57          shortURLDTO := hs.ShortURLService.ConvertShortURLToDTO(shortURL, hs.Cfg.AppURL)
    58          c.Logger.Debug("Created short URL", "url", shortURLDTO.URL)
    59
    60          return response.JSON(http.StatusOK, shortURLDTO)
    61  }

func (hs *HTTPServer) applyRoutes() {
        // start with middlewares & static routes
        hs.addMiddlewaresAndStaticRoutes()
        // then add view routes & api routes
        hs.RouteRegister.Register(hs.web, hs.namedMiddlewares...)
        // lastly not found route
        hs.web.NotFound(middleware.ProvideRouteOperationName("notfound"), middleware.ReqSignedIn, hs.NotFoundHandler)
}

func (hs *HTTPServer) addMiddlewaresAndStaticRoutes() {
        m := hs.web

        m.Use(requestmeta.SetupRequestMetadata())
        m.Use(middleware.RequestTracing(hs.tracer, middleware.SkipTracingPaths))
        m.Use(middleware.RequestMetrics(hs.Features, hs.Cfg, hs.promRegister))

        m.UseMiddleware(hs.LoggerMiddleware.Middleware())

        if hs.Cfg.EnableGzip {
                m.UseMiddleware(middleware.Gziper())
        }

        m.UseMiddleware(middleware.Recovery(hs.Cfg, hs.License))
        m.UseMiddleware(hs.Csrf.Middleware())

        hs.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, "build", "public/build")
        hs.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, "", "public", "/public/views/swagger.html")
        hs.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, "robots.txt", "robots.txt")
        hs.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, "mockServiceWorker.js", "mockServiceWorker.js")

        if hs.Cfg.ImageUploadProvider == "local" {
                hs.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.ImagesDir, "", "/public/img/attachments")
        }

        if len(hs.Cfg.CustomResponseHeaders) > 0 {
                m.Use(middleware.AddCustomResponseHeaders(hs.Cfg))
        }

        m.Use(middleware.AddDefaultResponseHeaders(hs.Cfg))

        if hs.Cfg.ServeFromSubPath && hs.Cfg.AppSubURL != "" {
                m.SetURLPrefix(hs.Cfg.AppSubURL)
                m.UseMiddleware(middleware.SubPathRedirect(hs.Cfg))
        }

        m.UseMiddleware(web.Renderer(filepath.Join(hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, "views"), "[[", "]]"))

        // These endpoints are used for monitoring the Grafana instance
        // and should not be redirected or rejected.
        m.Use(hs.healthzHandler)
        m.Use(hs.apiHealthHandler)
        m.Use(hs.metricsEndpoint)
        m.Use(hs.pluginMetricsEndpoint)
        m.Use(hs.frontendLogEndpoints())

        m.UseMiddleware(hs.ContextHandler.Middleware)
        m.Use(middleware.OrgRedirect(hs.Cfg, hs.userService))

        // needs to be after context handler
        if hs.Cfg.EnforceDomain {
                m.Use(middleware.ValidateHostHeader(hs.Cfg))
        }
        // handle action urls
        m.UseMiddleware(middleware.ValidateActionUrl(hs.Cfg, hs.log))

        m.Use(middleware.HandleNoCacheHeaders)

        if hs.Cfg.CSPEnabled || hs.Cfg.CSPReportOnlyEnabled {
                m.UseMiddleware(middleware.ContentSecurityPolicy(hs.Cfg, hs.log))
        }

        for _, mw := range hs.middlewares {
                m.Use(mw)
        }
}

func (hs *HTTPServer) metricsEndpoint(ctx *web.Context) {
        if !hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointEnabled {
                return
        }

                return
        }

        if hs.metricsEndpointBasicAuthEnabled() && !BasicAuthenticatedRequest(ctx.Req, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthUsername, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthPassword) {
                ctx.Resp.Header().Set("WWW-Authenticate", `Basic realm="Grafana"`)
                ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusUnauthorized)

// healthzHandler always return 200 - Ok if Grafana's web server is running
func (hs *HTTPServer) healthzHandler(ctx *web.Context) {
        notHeadOrGet := ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet && ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodHead
        if notHeadOrGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != "/healthz" {
                return
        }

        ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
        if _, err := ctx.Resp.Write([]byte("Ok")); err != nil {
                hs.log.Error("could not write to response", "err", err)
        }
}

// swagger:model healthResponse
type healthResponse struct {
        Database         string `json:"database"`
        Version          string `json:"version,omitempty"`
        Commit           string `json:"commit,omitempty"`
        EnterpriseCommit string `json:"enterpriseCommit,omitempty"`
}

// swagger:route GET /health health getHealth
//
// apiHealthHandler will return ok if Grafana's web server is running and it
// can access the database. If the database cannot be accessed it will return
// http status code 503.
//
// Responses:
// 200: healthResponse
// 503: internalServerError
func (hs *HTTPServer) apiHealthHandler(ctx *web.Context) {
        notHeadOrGet := ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet && ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodHead
        if notHeadOrGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != "/api/health" {
                return
        }

        data := healthResponse{
                Database: "ok",
        }
        if !hs.Cfg.Anonymous.HideVersion {
                data.Version = hs.Cfg.BuildVersion
                data.Commit = hs.Cfg.BuildCommit
                if hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit != "NA" && hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit != "" {
                        data.EnterpriseCommit = hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit
                }
        }

        if !hs.databaseHealthy(ctx.Req.Context()) {
                data.Database = "failing"
                ctx.Resp.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=UTF-8")
                ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
        } else {
                ctx.Resp.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=UTF-8")
                ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
        }

        dataBytes, err := json.MarshalIndent(data, "", "  ")
        if err != nil {
                hs.log.Error("Failed to encode data", "err", err)
                return
        }

        if _, err := ctx.Resp.Write(dataBytes); err != nil {
                hs.log.Error("Failed to write to response", "err", err)
        }
}

func (hs *HTTPServer) mapStatic(m *web.Mux, rootDir string, dir string, prefix string, exclude ...string) {
        headers := func(c *web.Context) {
                c.Resp.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=3600")
        }

        if prefix == "public/build" {
                headers = func(c *web.Context) {
                        c.Resp.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000")
                }
        }

        if hs.Cfg.Env == setting.Dev {
                headers = func(c *web.Context) {
                        c.Resp.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "max-age=0, must-revalidate, no-cache")
                }
        }

        if prefix == "mockServiceWorker.js" {
                headers = func(c *web.Context) {
                        c.Resp.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/javascript")
                }
        }

        m.Use(httpstatic.Static(
                path.Join(rootDir, dir),
                httpstatic.StaticOptions{
                        SkipLogging: true,
                        Prefix:      prefix,
                        AddHeaders:  headers,
                        Exclude:     exclude,
                },
        ))
}

    14  func (hs *HTTPServer) pluginMetricsEndpoint(ctx *web.Context) {
    15          if !hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointEnabled {
    16                  return
    17          }
    18
    19          if ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet || !strings.HasPrefix(ctx.Req.URL.Path, "/metrics/plugins/") {
    20                  return
    21          }
    22
    23          if hs.metricsEndpointBasicAuthEnabled() && !BasicAuthenticatedRequest(ctx.Req, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthUsername, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthPassword) {
    24                  ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusUnauthorized)
    25                  return
    26          }
    27
    28          pathParts := strings.SplitAfter(ctx.Req.URL.Path, "/")
    29          pluginID := pathParts[len(pathParts)-1]
    30
    31          resp, err := hs.pluginClient.CollectMetrics(ctx.Req.Context(), &backend.CollectMetricsRequest{PluginContext: backend.PluginContext{PluginID: pluginID}})
    32          if err != nil {
    33                  if errors.Is(err, plugins.ErrPluginNotRegistered) {
    34                          ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)
    35                          return
    36                  }
    37
    38                  ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
    39                  return
    40          }
    41
    42          ctx.Resp.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/plain")
    43          if _, err := ctx.Resp.Write(resp.PrometheusMetrics); err != nil {
    44                  hs.log.Error("Failed to write to response", "err", err)
    45          }
    46  }

    41                  accessToken, ok := web.Params(c.Req)[":accessToken"]

    56                          return
    57                  }

func (s *Service) exposeJWKS(ctx *contextmodel.ReqContext) response.Response {
        jwks, err := s.GetJWKS(ctx.Req.Context())
        if err != nil {
                s.log.Error("Failed to get JWKS", "err", err)
                return response.Error(http.StatusInternalServerError, "Failed to get JWKS", err)
        }

        // set cache headers to 1 hour
        ctx.Resp.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=3600")

        return response.JSON(http.StatusOK, jwks)
}

    60          if err := web.Bind(ctx.Req, &c); err != nil {
    61                  return response.Error(http.StatusBadRequest, "failed to parse request", err)
    62          }
    63
    64          bundle, err := s.create(context.Background(), c.Collectors, ctx.SignedInUser)
    65          if err != nil {
    66                  return response.Error(http.StatusInternalServerError, "failed to create support bundle", err)
    67          }
    68
    69          data, err := json.Marshal(bundle)
    70          if err != nil {
    71                  return response.Error(http.StatusInternalServerError, "failed to encode bundle", err)
    72          }
    73
    74          return response.JSON(http.StatusCreated, data)
    75  }

    19  func UploadErrorToStatusCode(err error) int {
    20          switch {
    21          case errors.Is(err, ErrStorageNotFound):
    22                  return 404
    23
    24          case errors.Is(err, ErrUnsupportedStorage):
    25                  return 400
    26
    27          case errors.Is(err, ErrValidationFailed):
    28                  return 400
    29
    30          case errors.Is(err, ErrQuotaReached):
    31                  return 400
    32
    33          case errors.Is(err, ErrFileAlreadyExists):
    34                  return 400
    35
    36          case errors.Is(err, ErrAccessDenied):
    37                  return 403
    38
    39          default:
    40                  return 500
    41          }
    42  }

    60  func (s *standardStorageService) doWrite(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) response.Response {
    61          scope, path := getPathAndScope(c)
    62          cmd := &WriteValueRequest{}
    63          if err := web.Bind(c.Req, cmd); err != nil {
    64                  return response.Error(http.StatusBadRequest, "bad request data", err)
    65          }
    66          cmd.Path = scope + "/" + path
    67          rsp, err := s.write(c.Req.Context(), c.SignedInUser, cmd)
    68          if err != nil {
    69                  return response.Error(http.StatusBadRequest, "save error", err)
    70          }
    71          return response.JSON(http.StatusOK, rsp)
    72  }

// NoAuth creates a middleware that doesn't require any authentication.
// If forceLogin param is set it will redirect the user to the login page.
func NoAuth() web.Handler {
        return func(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) {
                if shouldForceLogin(c) {
                        notAuthorized(c)
                        return
                }
        }
}

// shouldForceLogin checks if user should be enforced to login.
// Returns true if forceLogin parameter is set.
func shouldForceLogin(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) bool {
        forceLogin := false
        forceLoginParam, err := strconv.ParseBool(c.Req.URL.Query().Get("forceLogin"))
        if err == nil {
                forceLogin = forceLoginParam
        }

        return forceLogin

// SnapshotPublicModeOrCreate creates a middleware that allows access
// if snapshot public mode is enabled or if user has creation permission.
func SnapshotPublicModeOrCreate(cfg *setting.Cfg, ac2 ac.AccessControl) web.Handler {
        return func(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) {
                if cfg.SnapshotPublicMode {
                        return
                }

                if !c.IsSignedIn {
                        notAuthorized(c)
                        return
                }

                ac.Middleware(ac2)(ac.EvalPermission(dashboards.ActionSnapshotsCreate))
        }
}

// SnapshotPublicModeOrDelete creates a middleware that allows access
// if snapshot public mode is enabled or if user has delete permission.
func SnapshotPublicModeOrDelete(cfg *setting.Cfg, ac2 ac.AccessControl) web.Handler {
        return func(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) {
                if cfg.SnapshotPublicMode {
                        return
                }

                if !c.IsSignedIn {
                        notAuthorized(c)
                        return
                }

                ac.Middleware(ac2)(ac.EvalPermission(dashboards.ActionSnapshotsDelete))
        }
}

func ReqNotSignedIn(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) {
        if c.IsSignedIn {
                c.Redirect(setting.AppSubUrl + "/")
        }
}

func (l *LibraryElementService) registerAPIEndpoints() {

func (l *LibraryElementService) getByNameHandler(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) response.Response {
        elements, err := l.getLibraryElementsByName(c.Req.Context(), c.SignedInUser, web.Params(c.Req)[":name"])
        if err != nil {
                return l.toLibraryElementError(err, "Failed to get library element")
        }

        filteredElements, err := l.filterLibraryPanelsByPermission(c, elements)
        if err != nil {
                return l.toLibraryElementError(err, err.Error())
        }

        return response.JSON(http.StatusOK, model.LibraryElementArrayResponse{Result: filteredElements})
}

func (l *LibraryElementService) filterLibraryPanelsByPermission(c *contextmodel.ReqContext, elements []model.LibraryElementDTO) ([]model.LibraryElementDTO, error) {
        filteredPanels := make([]model.LibraryElementDTO, 0)
        for _, p := range elements {
                allowed, err := l.AccessControl.Evaluate(c.Req.Context(), c.SignedInUser, ac.EvalPermission(ActionLibraryPanelsRead, ScopeLibraryPanelsProvider.GetResourceScopeUID(p.UID)))
                if err != nil {
                        // This could fail because the folder that contains the library panel does not exist or the user doesn't have permissions to read it.
                        // We skip it instead of breaking the library panel list rendering flow and log the error.
                        l.log.Warn("Failed to evaluate permissions", "error", err)
                        continue
                }
                if allowed {
                        filteredPanels = append(filteredPanels, p)
                }
        }

        return filteredPanels, nil

func (hs *HTTPServer) applyRoutes() {
        // start with middlewares & static routes
        hs.addMiddlewaresAndStaticRoutes()
        // then add view routes & api routes
        hs.RouteRegister.Register(hs.web, hs.namedMiddlewares...)
        // lastly not found route
        hs.web.NotFound(middleware.ProvideRouteOperationName("notfound"), middleware.ReqSignedIn, hs.NotFoundHandler)
}

func (hs *HTTPServer) addMiddlewaresAndStaticRoutes() {
        m := hs.web

        m.Use(requestmeta.SetupRequestMetadata())
        m.Use(middleware.RequestTracing(hs.tracer, middleware.SkipTracingPaths))
        m.Use(middleware.RequestMetrics(hs.Features, hs.Cfg, hs.promRegister))

        m.UseMiddleware(hs.LoggerMiddleware.Middleware())

        if hs.Cfg.EnableGzip {
                m.UseMiddleware(middleware.Gziper())
        }

        m.UseMiddleware(middleware.Recovery(hs.Cfg, hs.License))
...

                ServeHTTP(ctx.Resp, ctx.Req)
}

// healthzHandler always return 200 - Ok if Grafana's web server is running
func (hs *HTTPServer) healthzHandler(ctx *web.Context) {
        notHeadOrGet := ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet && ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodHead
        if notHeadOrGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != "/healthz" {
                return
        }

        ctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
        if _, err := ctx.Resp.Write([]byte("Ok")); err != nil {
                hs.log.Error("could not write to response", "err", err)
        }
}

#199 assistant/tool_use execute_bash
#200 user/tool_result execute_bash
func (s *Service) exposeJWKS(ctx *contextmodel.ReqContext) response.Response {
        jwks, err := s.GetJWKS(ctx.Req.Context())
        if err != nil {
                s.log.Error("Failed to get JWKS", "err", err)
                return response.Error(http.StatusInternalServerError, "Failed to get JWKS", err)
        }

        // set cache headers to 1 hour
        ctx.Resp.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=3600")

        return response.JSON(http.StatusOK, jwks)
}

   150          s.routeGet(m, "/metrics", promhttp.HandlerFor(s.promGatherer, promhttp.HandlerOpts{EnableOpenMetrics: true}))
   151
   152          // Frontend service doesn't (yet?) serve any assets, so explicitly 404
   153          // them so we can get logs for them
   154          s.routeGet(m, "/public/*", http.NotFound)
   155
   156          // Empty health check endpoint to allow k8s and other orchestrators to check if the server is alive
   157          // Useful to have a separate route for this for logging and metrics purposes
   158          s.routeGet(m, "/-/health", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   159                  w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
   160
   161                  if _, err := w.Write([]byte("OK")); err != nil {
   162                          s.log.Error("failed to write health check response", "error", err)
   163                  }
   164          })
   165
   166          // Frontend boot error reporting endpoint
   167          s.routePost(m, "/-/fe-boot-error", s.handleBootError)

// NoAuth creates a middleware that doesn't require any authentication.
// If forceLogin param is set it will redirect the user to the login page.
func NoAuth() web.Handler {
        return func(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) {
                if shouldForceLogin(c) {
                        notAuthorized(c)
                        return
                }
        }
}

// shouldForceLogin checks if user should be enforced to login.
// Returns true if forceLogin parameter is set.
func shouldForceLogin(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) bool {
        forceLogin := false
        forceLoginParam, err := strconv.ParseBool(c.Req.URL.Query().Get("forceLogin"))
        if err == nil {
                forceLogin = forceLoginParam
        }

        return forceLogin

// SnapshotPublicModeOrCreate creates a middleware that allows access
// if snapshot public mode is enabled or if user has creation permission.
func SnapshotPublicModeOrCreate(cfg *setting.Cfg, ac2 ac.AccessControl) web.Handler {
        return func(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) {
                if cfg.SnapshotPublicMode {
                        return
                }

                if !c.IsSignedIn {
                        notAuthorized(c)
                        return
                }

                ac.Middleware(ac2)(ac.EvalPermission(dashboards.ActionSnapshotsCreate))
        }
}

// SnapshotPublicModeOrDelete creates a middleware that allows access
// if snapshot public mode is enabled or if user has delete permission.
func SnapshotPublicModeOrDelete(cfg *setting.Cfg, ac2 ac.AccessControl) web.Handler {
        return func(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) {
                if cfg.SnapshotPublicMode {
                        return
                }

                if !c.IsSignedIn {
                        notAuthorized(c)
                        return
                }

                ac.Middleware(ac2)(ac.EvalPermission(dashboards.ActionSnapshotsDelete))
        }
}

func (l *LibraryElementService) getByNameHandler(c *contextmodel.ReqContext) response.Response {
        elements, err := l.getLibraryElementsByName(c.Req.Context(), c.SignedInUser, web.Params(c.Req)[":name"])
        if err != nil {
                return l.toLibraryElementError(err, "Failed to get library element")
        }

        filteredElements, err := l.filterLibraryPanelsByPermission(c, elements)
        if err != nil {
                return l.toLibraryElementError(err, err.Error())
        }

        return response.JSON(http.StatusOK, model.LibraryElementArrayResponse{Result: filteredElements})
}
